## Coverage matrix and next review
| Acquisition cluster | Existing starting pages | Next review question |
|---|---|---|
| Capital and funding | high-income guide; stock-sale and business-sale guides; purchase-budget calculator | Does the cash-versus-debt comparison answer a high-liquidity buyer's actual acquisition choice? Review delayed-financing-cash-str before creating another page. |
| Income and tax suitability | str-tax-savings-guide; when-str-tax-strategy-does-not-fit; tax-benefit-shortfall guide | Do profession pages distinguish personal suitability, available cash and property purchase steps without promised savings? |
| Property search | how-to-find-str-properties-for-sale; short-term-rental-buy-box | Are the shortlist evidence requirements complete for a remote, purchase-ready investor? |
| Underwriting and offer | reconcile-airbnb-payout-export; str-maximum-offer-price-from-revenue; compare-two-str-properties-before-offer | Are seller records, downside costs and an offer decision supported by worked examples? |
| Permissions and diligence | permits-that-do-not-transfer; str-due-diligence-checklist | Are jurisdiction-specific statements current and linked to primary sources? |
| Lender and ownership | str-mortgage-preapproval-before-property; dscr-loans-for-airbnb; str-partnership-structure | Do partnership funding, signer and reserve questions have distinct complete answers? Read relevant existing pages first. |
| Closing and rental readiness | str-purchase-to-launch-timeline; str-placed-in-service-date | Are delay costs and readiness evidence usable at an actual diligence deadline? |
| Operating responsibilities | management; str-investing-for-physicians; spouse-material-participation | Does each acquisition handoff identify real owner/manager work rather than generic guest advice? |
| Acquisition service choice | pricing; bnb-accelerator-vs-diy; str-buyer-agent-vs-acquisition-team | Can a buyer reconcile a provider quote with the all-in property budget? Review pricing and existing fee articles before adding a distinct quote-comparison guide. |
| Holding and exit | cost-seg-timing-and-hold-period; 1031-exchange-short-term-rental | Are exit liquidity, basis and future obligations treated as adviser-specific inputs rather than guaranteed deferral? |

## Publication gate
Read the relevant existing page before deciding a gap exists. Add an article only for a materially distinct purchase decision. Include original analysis, a worksheet, a comparison or a specific evidence checklist. Verify changing financial, legal and tax facts from primary sources. Clearly label hypothetical figures. Preserve honest authorship and dates. Do not generate city, occupation, tax-bill or capital-amount swaps for volume.
